Result: 当前模型常默认为查表操作，难以识别预测意图；准确的意图消歧是引导模型执行预测行为的前提；提升预测精度的上限需要集成更复杂的建模或推理能力。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在表格隐式预测问答中表现不佳，意图识别是关键瓶颈，未来需加强复杂推理与建模能力的整合以提升预测性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced Table Question Answering, where most queries can be answered by extracting information or simple aggregation. However, a common class of real-world queries is implicitly predictive, requiring the inference of unobserved answers from historical patterns rather than mere retrieval. These queries introduce two challenges: recognizing latent intent and reliable predictive reasoning over massive tables. To assess LLMs in such Tabular questiOn answering with implicit Prediction tasks, we introduce TopBench, a benchmark consisting of 779 samples across four sub-tasks, ranging from single-point prediction to decision making, treatment effect analysis, and complex filtering, requiring models to generate outputs spanning reasoning text and structured tables. We evaluate diverse models under both text-based and agentic workflows. Experiments reveal that current models often struggle with intent recognition, defaulting to just lookups. Deeper analysis identifies that accurate intent disambiguation serves as the prerequisite for leading these predictive behaviors. Furthermore, elevating the upper bound of prediction precision requires the integration of more sophisticated modeling or reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [112] [On the Proper Treatment of Units in Surprisal Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28147)
*Samuel Kiegeland,Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson,Tim Vieira,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出surprisal理论中“单位”定义不明确的问题，将分析单位的选择与兴趣区域的选择区分开来，提出了一个统一框架来处理任意单位清单上的surprisal，并主张将tokenization视为实现细节而非科学基元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有surprisal研究在处理语言单位时缺乏明确规范，实验刺激被分割为语言单位（如词），而预训练语言模型将概率分配给固定的token字母表，两者通常不对齐。这导致surprisal预测隐式依赖于临时性程序，混淆了分析单位定义和评估兴趣区域选择这两个不同建模决策。

Method: 提出了一个统一框架，将分析单位的定义与兴趣区域的选择进行解耦，使surprisal能够在任意单位清单上进行推理和处理。

Result: 揭示了当前实践中surprisal预测因单位不一致而产生的混淆问题，并给出了解耦后的形式化框架。

Conclusion: surprisal分析应明确这些选择，并将tokenization视为实现细节而非科学基元。

Abstract: Surprisal theory links human processing effort to the predictability of an upcoming linguistic unit, but empirical work often leaves the notion of a unit underspecified. In practice, experimental stimuli are segmented into linguistically motivated units (e.g., words), while pretrained language models assign probability mass to a fixed token alphabet that typically does not align with those units. As a result, surprisal-based predictors depend implicitly on ad hoc procedures that conflate two distinct modeling choices: the definition of the unit of analysis and the choice of regions of interest over which predictions are evaluated. In this paper, we disentangle these choices and give a unified framework for reasoning about surprisal over arbitrary unit inventories. We argue that surprisal-based analyses should make these choices explicit and treat tokenization as an implementation detail rather than a scientific primitive.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [A Randomized Controlled Trial and Pilot of Scout: an LLM-Based EHR Search and Synthesis Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26953)
*Michael Gao,Suresh Balu,William Knechtle,Kartik Pejavara,William Jeck,Matthew Ellis,Jason Thieling,Blake Cameron,Jason Tatreau,Tareq Aljurf,Henry Foote,Michael Revoir,Marshall Nichols,Matthew Gardner,William Ratliff,Bradley Hintze,Angelo Milazzo,Sreekanth Vemulapalli*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于LLM的EHR搜索与综合平台Scout，通过前瞻性随机交叉试验（20名参与者、200个结构化病例）证明其可将任务完成时间减少37.6%，显著降低感知工作负荷，同时保持准确性、完整性和相关性。超过200名用户、20多个专科的试点部署产生了6600多次交互，自动化评估显示低错误率，人工审查发现大多数被标记的错误实际有病历支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）中的临床文档和数据检索是导致临床医生工作负荷增加和职业倦怠的重要因素，亟需开发能够减轻这一负担的有效工具。

Method: 开发了Scout这一基于LLM的EHR搜索与综合平台；开展了前瞻性随机、评估者盲法交叉试验（7个临床专科、20名参与者、200个结构化病例）；进行了超过200名用户、20多个专科的试点部署；采用LLM-as-judge框架进行自动化评估并结合人工复核。

Result: 任务完成时间减少37.6%；NASA-TLX工作负荷显著降低，尤其在心理需求、努力和时间需求方面；非劣效性分析表明准确性、完整性和相关性未降低；试点部署产生6600多次交互；自动化评估错误率低，人工审查发现大多数标记错误实际有病历支持。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的EHR工具能够在保持输出质量的同时，有效减少临床和行政工作负荷，提供了早期试验层面的证据。

Abstract: Clinical documentation and data retrieval within Electronic Health Records (EHRs) contribute substantially to clinician workload and burnout. To address this, we developed Scout, an LLM-based EHR search and synthesis platform that enables clinicians to query EHR data using natural language. Each response includes citations linking each claim to the original data source, facilitating easy verification of generated content. We conducted a prospective randomized, evaluator-blinded crossover trial across seven clinical specialties (20 participants, 200 structured cases). Participants completed realistic clinical tasks using either Scout or the EHR alone, with outcomes including time to completion, NASA Task Load Index workload scores, and blinded expert adjudication of accuracy, completeness, and relevance. Scout reduced task completion time by 37.6% and significantly decreased perceived workload, with the largest reductions in mental demand, effort, and temporal demand. Non-inferiority analyses showed that tasks completed with Scout maintained accuracy, completeness, and relevance relative to tasks completed with the EHR-only. A concurrent pilot deployment across over 200 users and more than 20 specialties generated over 6,600 interactions in three months, revealing diverse clinical and administrative use cases. Automated evaluation using an LLM-as-judge framework identified errors at low rates. Subsequent manual review of a subset of outputs revealed that most claims flagged by the automated judge as errors were in fact supported by the patient chart, demonstrating the importance of human validation. These findings provide early trial-based evidence that LLM-powered EHR tools can meaningfully reduce clinical and administrative workloads while maintaining output quality.

</details>


### [114] [AgenticRecTune: Multi-Agent with Self-Evolving Skillhub for Recommendation System Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26969)
*Xidong Wu,Yue Zhuan,Ruoqiao Wei,Hangxin Chen,Di Bai,Jintao Liu,Xinyi Wang,Xue Wang,Luoshu Wang,Xinwu Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticRecTune，一个基于LLM（Gemini）的多智能体框架，用于自动化推荐系统多阶段流水线中系统级配置的优化，包含Actor、Critic、Insight、Skill、Online五个专门智能体，通过协同工作完成配置空间探索、过滤、A/B测试及经验积累。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统多阶段流水线中系统级配置优化至关重要，但面临三大挑战：每次模型修改都需要重新优化配置，迭代成本高；不同阶段模型目标各异，需要跨领域专业知识；需要平衡多个在线指标并适应动态开发目标。现有研究多聚焦单模型优化，缺乏对系统级配置自动化的有效方案。

Method: 提出五智能体框架：Actor Agent利用LLM推理探索最优配置空间并生成多个候选配置；Critic Agent过滤次优配置；Online Agent基于筛选结果自动准备A/B测试并捕获实验结果；Insight Agent与Skill Agent协作构建自进化Skillhub，总结历史结果、提取推荐系统中各任务的底层机制并更新技能库。

Result: 利用Gemini大语言模型的高级推理能力，实现了端到端的配置优化工作流自动化，能够自主探索最优配置空间、执行A/B实验并持续从历史经验中学习。

Conclusion: AgenticRecTune通过多智能体协作和LLM驱动的推理能力，有效解决了推荐系统多阶段流水线中系统级配置优化的复杂性问题，实现了自动化和持续改进的配置优化流程。

Abstract: Modern large-scale recommendation systems are typically constructed as multi-stage pipelines, encompassing pre-ranking, ranking, and re-ranking phases. While traditional recommendation research typically focuses on optimizing a specific model, such as improving the pre-ranking model structure or ranking models training algorithm, system-level configurations optimization play a crucial role, which integrates the output from each model head to get the final score in each stage. Due to the complexity of the system, the configuration optimization is highly important and challenging. Any model modification requires new optimal system-level configurations. But each experimental iteration requires significant tuning effort. Furthermore, models in different stage operates within a distinct context and optimizes for different targets, requiring specialized domain expertise. In addition, optimization success depends on balancing competing multiple online metrics and alignment with shifting production development objectives. To address these challenges, we propose AgenticRecTune, an agentic framework comprising five specialized agents, Actor, Critic, Insight, Skill, and Online, designed to manage the end-to-end configuration optimization workflow. By leveraging the advanced reasoning of Large Language Models (LLMs), specifically Gemini, AgenticRecTune explore the optimal configuration spaces. The Actor Agent proposes multiple candidates and Critic Agent filters out suboptimal proposals.Then Online Agent autonomously prepares A/B tests based on the proposed configurations set from the Critic Agent and captures the subsequencet experimental results. We also introduce a self-evolving Skillhub, which utilizes a collaboration between the Insight Agent and Skill Agent to summarize the history results, extract underlying mechanics of each task in recommendation system and update skills.

</details>


### [115] [Not All Memories Age the Same: Autodiscovery of Adaptive Decay in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26970)
*Mandar Karhade*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 现有知识图谱检索将所有事实视为同等时效，本文提出层次化连续衰减框架，通过速度（观测频率）和波动性（嵌入距离变化）两个正交信号参数化衰减曲面，并在领域、上下文和实体三个层次上学习，实验验证了该方法的有效性，均匀衰减比无时间加权效果差18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱检索将所有事实视为同等时效，而现有时序方法使用统一的遗忘曲线，忽略了不同知识类型具有不同的时间动态特性。核心问题并非延迟或吞吐量，而是在查询时识别什么信息是重要的。

Method: 提出层次化框架，用速度（观测频率）和波动性（嵌入距离变化）两个正交信号参数化连续衰减曲面，分解为三个可学习层次：领域级（通用模式）、上下文级（环境相关变化）和实体级（个性化衰减），通过生存分析从数据中学习参数。

Result: 合成时序知识图谱实验成功恢复层次化参数（HDBSCAN ARI=1.0）；在107篇维基百科文章和1163个临床记录上验证，速度-波动性簇自然涌现且对齐可观测持久性模式，近乎普遍存在林迪效应（Weibull形状参数k<1）。均匀衰减比无时间加权效果差18倍，层次化衰减逐步改善。

Conclusion: 不同知识类型具有不同的时间动态特性，统一衰减方法严重不足；提出的层次化连续衰减框架能有效捕捉这些差异，显著优于均匀衰减和无时间加权方法。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs used for retrieval treat all facts as equally current. Existing temporal approaches apply uniform decay, using a single forgetting curve regardless of knowledge type. We show this is fundamentally misspecified: different knowledge types exhibit different temporal dynamics, and the core retrieval problem is not latency or throughput but identifying what is important at query time.
  We propose a hierarchical framework that replaces uniform decay with a continuous decay surface parameterized by two orthogonal signals: velocity (how frequently a concept is observed) and volatility (how much the value changes between observations, measured via embedding distance). The decay surface is decomposed into three learnable levels: domain-level parameters capture universal patterns (some predicates are inherently permanent, others inherently transient), context-level parameters capture setting-dependent variation, and entity-level adaptation personalizes decay to specific subjects. All parameters emerge from data through survival analysis on observed value lifetimes, requiring no predefined taxonomies or domain expertise.
  We formulate edge lifetime as a survival problem where the event is value supersession (a meaningfully different value replacing the current one), distinct from mere re-observation. Experiments on synthetic temporal knowledge graphs demonstrate recovery of planted hierarchical parameters (HDBSCAN ARI = 1.0).
  Validation on 107 Wikipedia articles and 1,163 patient records from the Synthea clinical EHR simulator shows that velocity-volatility clusters emerge naturally, align with observable persistence patterns, and near-universally exhibit the Lindy effect (Weibull shape k < 1). Uniform decay performs 18x worse than no temporal weighting. Heterogeneous decay recovers from this, with each hierarchy level contributing measurable improvement.

</details>


### [116] [T2S-Metrics: Unified Library for Evaluating SPARQL Queries Generated From Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26971)
*Yousouf Taghzouti,Tao Jiang,Camille Juigné,Benjamin Navet,Fabien Gandon,Franck Michel,Louis-Felix Nothias*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 t2s-metrics，一个开源的、可扩展的统一评估库，专门用于 SPARQL 查询比较和基于执行的评估，涵盖超过20种评估指标，包括词法、句法、语义、结构、执行和排序维度，旨在解决知识图谱问答系统评估中的碎片化、不一致和低可重复性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱问答系统的评估长期存在碎片化、不一致和可重复性差的问题。现有基准通常只关注少量指标（如查询精确匹配或答案级F1），忽略了语法有效性、语义忠实度、执行正确性、结果排序质量和计算效率，缺乏统一、标准化的评估框架。

Method: t2s-metrics 是一个开源、可扩展的统一评估库，提供超过20种评估指标，涵盖词法级（token级Precision/Recall/F1、BLEU、ROUGE、METEOR、CodeBLEU）、句法级（变量归一化的SP-BLEU、SP-F1）、语义级（图和URI精确匹配）、执行级（F1-QALD、Jaccard相似度）、排序级（MRR、NDCG、P@k、Hit@k）以及LLM-as-a-Judge指标。采用模块化抽象层解耦指标规范与实现，受 ir-metrics 库启发设计。

Result: t2s-metrics 提供了统一、可扩展的评估框架，支持从多个维度对 SPARQL 查询及其执行结果进行全面评估，实现了评估的可重复性和透明度。

Conclusion: t2s-metrics 是迈向知识图谱问答系统系统化、标准化评估的必要一步，能够超越单纯的答案正确性，提供对系统行为更深层次的诊断性洞察。

Abstract: The evaluation of Question Answering (QA) systems over Knowledge Graphs has historically suffered from fragmentation, inconsistency, and limited reproducibility. While significant progress has been made in semantic parsing and SPARQL query generation, evaluation methodologies remain diverse, ad hoc, and often incomparable across studies. Existing benchmarks typically focus on a small subset of metrics, such as query exact match or answer-level F1, neglecting syntactic validity, semantic faithfulness, execution correctness, results ranking quality, and computational efficiency. In this paper, we present t2s-metrics, an open-source, extensible, and unified evaluation library designed specifically for SPARQL query comparison and execution-based assessment. t2s-metrics provides a broad and extensible set of over 20 evaluation metrics, collected from the literature and practical evaluation needs, spanning lexical, syntactic, semantic, structural, execution-based and ranking-based dimensions. These include query-based metrics such as token-level Precision, Recall, and F1; BLEU, ROUGE, METEOR, and CodeBLEU variants; variable-normalized metrics (SP-BLEU, SP-F1); graph-and URI-based exact match metrics; as well as answer set-based metrics such as F1-QALD and Jaccard similarity; ranking metrics including MRR, NDCG, P@k, and Hit@k; and LLM-as-a-Judge metrics. Taking inspiration from the ir-metrics library for Information Retrieval, t2s-metrics provides a modular abstraction layer that decouples metric specification from implementation, enabling consistent, transparent, and reproducible evaluation of SPARQLbased QA systems. We argue that t2s-metrics constitutes a necessary step toward systematic, standardized evaluation in question answering over knowledge graphs and facilitates deeper diagnostic insights into system behavior beyond answer correctness.

</details>


### [117] [Budget-Constrained Online Retrieval-Augmented Generation: The Chunk-as-a-Service Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26981)
*Shawqi Al-Maliki,Ammar Gharaibeh,Mohamed Rahouti,Mohammad Ruhul Amin,Mohamed Abdallah,Junaid Qadir,Ala Al-Fuqaha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Chunk-as-a-Service (CaaS)作为RAG-as-a-Service (RaaS)的透明且经济高效的替代方案，包含两个变体：Open-Budget CaaS (OB-CaaS)和Limited-Budget CaaS (LB-CaaS)。LB-CaaS通过提出的Utility-Cost Online Selection Algorithm (UCOSA)实现在线子集选择，基于预算约束和效用-成本权衡来丰富提示词。实验表明，UCOSA在NEP×AR指标上比随机选择高约52%，达到离线选择的约75%；LB-CaaS和OB-CaaS的性能-预算比分别比RaaS高140%和86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG-as-a-Service存在不透明且成本效益低的问题：定价基于提交提示词数量，不考虑是否被相关块丰富或块的质量，导致不透明且成本效率低的支付模式。

Method: 提出Chunk-as-a-Service (CaaS)框架，包括OB-CaaS和LB-CaaS两个变体；设计了Utility-Cost Online Selection Algorithm (UCOSA)，该算法在线地根据预算约束和效用-成本权衡选择子集提示词进行丰富。

Result: UCOSA在NEP×AR指标上比随机选择高约52%，达到离线选择方法的约75%；LB-CaaS和OB-CaaS的性能-预算比分别比RaaS高140%和86%。

Conclusion: 所提出的CaaS及其算法UCOSA在透明性、成本效益和预算利用率上显著优于现有RaaS方案，验证了其作为替代方案的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized the field of natural language processing. However, they exhibit some limitations, including a lack of reliability and transparency: they may hallucinate and fail to provide sources that support the generated output. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) was introduced to address such limitations in LLMs. One popular implementation, RAG-as-a-Service (RaaS), has shortcomings that hinder its adoption and accessibility. For instance, RaaS pricing is based on the number of submitted prompts, without considering whether the prompts are enriched by relevant chunks, i.e., text segments retrieved from a vector database, or the quality of the utilized chunks (i.e., their degree of relevance). This results in an opaque and less cost-effective payment model. We propose Chunk-as-a-Service (CaaS) as a transparent and cost-effective alternative. CaaS includes two variants: Open-Budget CaaS (OB-CaaS) and Limited-Budget CaaS (LB-CaaS), which is enabled by our ``Utility-Cost Online Selection Algorithm (UCOSA)''. UCOSA further extends the cost-effectiveness and the accessibility of the OB-CaaS variant by enriching, in an online manner, a subset of the submitted prompts based on budget constraints and utility-cost tradeoff. Our experiments demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed UCOSA compared to both offline and relevance-greedy selection baselines. In terms of the performance metric-the number of enriched prompts (NEP) multiplied by the Average Relevance (AR)-UCOSA outperforms random selection by approximately 52% and achieves around 75% of the performance of offline selection methods. Additionally, in terms of budget utilization, LB-CaaS and OB-CaaS achieve higher performance-to-budget ratios of 140% and 86%, respectively, compared to RaaS, indicating their superior efficiency.

</details>


### [118] [Value-Aware Product Recommendation by Customer Segmentation using a suitable High-Dimensional Similarity Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26983)
*María Florencia Acosta,Rodrigo García Arancibia,Pamela Llop,Mariel Lovatto,Lucas Mansilla*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种价值感知的产品推荐方法，通过在用户-物品矩阵中编码收入贡献并使用基于收入的相似度度量进行用户分群，同时解决了高维稀疏问题并融入了收入和利润目标，在仿真和UCI在线零售数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统主要关注用户偏好匹配，忽视了产品及用户对销售收入的实际贡献；同时面临用户-物品数据的高维稀疏问题，缺乏将盈利能力直接融入推荐过程的系统性方法。

Method: 在用户-物品矩阵中编码收入贡献信息，基于此计算用户相似度并实现用户分群；对比传统相似度度量与适应高维场景的新型度量；提出基于收入份额、产品流行度和预期利润的三种推荐策略。

Result: 通过仿真实验和UCI Online Retail数据集的真实应用验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的价值感知推荐框架能够有效将收入贡献融入推荐过程，在保持推荐质量的同时实现与盈利目标对齐的推荐，为面向商业收益的推荐系统提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel value-aware approach to product recommendation that simultaneously addresses the high dimensionality and sparsity of user-item data while explicitly incorporating the contribution of each product and user to overall sales revenue. The proposed framework encodes revenue contributions in the user-item matrix and computes customer similarity directly on this basis using suitable distance measures. This enables the segmentation of users according to the revenue-based similarity of their purchase baskets and supports recommendations aligned with profitability objectives. We compare conventional similarity metrics with a novel alternative tailored to high-dimensional contexts and propose three recommendation strategies based on revenue share, product popularity, and expected profit generation. The effectiveness of the proposed method is validated through simulation experiments and a real-world application using the UCI Online Retail dataset.

</details>


### [119] [LUCid: Redefining Relevance For Lifelong Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26996)
*Chimaobi Okite,Anika Misra,Joyce Chai,Rada Mihalcea*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LUCid是一个评估个性化系统中情境化用户相关性（而非语义相关性）的基准测试，包含1,936个查询和最多500轮交互历史。实验表明，当相关上下文来自语义遥远的交互历史时，最先进的模型（如Gemini-3-Flash、GPT-5.4、Claude Haiku）性能急剧下降，检索召回率近乎为零，响应对齐度仅约50%，揭示了当前系统对相关性的定义与个性化所需的情境相关性之间存在根本性错配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身个性化方法通过语义邻近性来定义相关性，导致它们无法从主题不相关的用户交互中捕捉重要的用户信息，存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 构建LUCid基准测试，包含1,936个与现实场景匹配的查询和来自最多500个会话的交互历史，系统性评估多种架构在情境化用户相关性检索上的表现。

Result: 在最具挑战性的实例上，语义遥远历史中的相关上下文检索召回率降至接近零；即使是最先进模型（Gemini-3-Flash、GPT-5.4、Claude Haiku），响应对齐度也仅约50%。

Conclusion: 当前系统的相关性定义与个性化所需的情境相关性之间存在根本性错配，这直接影响了个性化系统的鲁棒性和安全性，LUCid为重新校准个性化与用户中心相关性提供了评估工具。

Abstract: Current approaches to lifelong personalization operationalize relevance through semantic proximity, causing them to miss essential user information from topically unrelated interactions. To address this gap, we introduce LUCid, a benchmark designed to measure situational user-centric relevance in personalization. The benchmark consists of 1,936 realistic queries paired with interaction histories from up to 500 sessions. Across multiple architectures, our experiments show significant performance collapse when relevant context must be surfaced from semantically distant history: retrieval recall drops to near zero on the hardest instances, and response alignment remains near 50% even for state-of-the-art models such as Gemini-3-Flash, GPT-5.4, and Claude Haiku. These results expose a fundamental mismatch between the notion of relevance encoded by current systems and the situational relevance required for personalization, with direct implications for robustness and safety when critical user attributes remain undetected. LUCid enables the systematic evaluation of whether current models can surface situationally-relevant user information from previous interactions, and serves as a step toward realigning personalization with user-centered relevance.

</details>


### [120] [A Gated Hybrid Contrastive Collaborative Filtering Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27117)
*Eduardo Ferreira da Silva,Mayki dos Santos Oliveira,Joel Machado Pires,Denis Dantas Boaventura,Maycon Maciel Peixoto,Cassio Serafim Prazeres,Gustavo Bittencourt Figueiredo,Miriam Capretz,Frederico Araujo Durão*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种门控混合协同过滤框架（Gated Hybrid Collaborative Filtering），通过自适应门控机制将评论文本语义注入自动编码器协同模型，并引入对比学习对齐语义与协同信号，使用成对贝叶斯个性化排序损失优化Top-N推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评论的推荐模型主要针对评分预测优化，而非排序质量，导致其在Top-N推荐场景中效果有限。

Method: 1) 自动编码器协同过滤框架；2) 自适应门控机制逐层注入语义信号；3) 对比学习模块对齐语义与协同信号；4) 成对贝叶斯个性化排序（BPR）损失函数。

Result: 在Amazon Movies & TV、IMDb和Rotten Tomatoes数据集上，Hit Rate@10和NDCG@10均优于现有基于评论的基线方法。

Conclusion: 受控的语义融合对排序驱动的推荐任务至关重要，门控机制和对比学习能够有效提升推荐排序质量。

Abstract: Recommender systems increasingly incorporate textual reviews to enrich user and item representations. However, most review-aware models remain optimized for rating prediction rather than ranking quality. This misalignment limits their effectiveness in top-N recommendation scenarios, where discriminative ranking is essential. To address this gap, we propose a Gated Hybrid Collaborative Filtering framework that integrates review-derived representations into an autoencoder-based collaborative model. The architecture injects semantic signals layer-wise through an adaptive gating mechanism that dynamically balances collaborative embeddings and topic-based features during encoding. To further refine the latent space, we introduce a contrastive learning module that aligns semantic and collaborative signals. We evaluate the framework across five distinct configurations: Pure collaborative; Topic and Gated; Text and Gated; and the addition of contrastive objectives (Contrastive and Topic, and Contrastive and Text). To explicitly optimize ranking behavior, the model is trained with a pairwise Bayesian personalized ranking objective, which promotes separation between relevant and non-relevant items in the latent space. Experiments on Amazon Movies & TV, IMDb, and Rotten Tomatoes demonstrate consistent improvements in hit rate @10 and normalized discounted cumulative gain @10 over state-of-the-art review-aware baselines. Results highlight the importance of controlled semantic fusion for ranking-driven recommendation.

</details>


### [121] [LLM-Enhanced Topical Trend Detection at Snapchat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27131)
*Hangqi Zhao,Jay Li,Abhiruchi Bhattacharya,Cong Ni,Jason Yeung,Jinchao Ye,Kai Yang,Akshat Malu,Manish Malik*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模系统，用于在Snapchat短视频平台上自动检测新兴话题趋势。该系统整合了多模态主题提取、时间序列突发检测和基于LLM的整合与增强，实现了准确及时的趋势发现。经过六个月持续离线人工评估，系统在识别有意义趋势方面表现出高精度，并已全球部署应用于内容排序和搜索等下游场景，显著提升了内容新鲜度和用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自动检测社交媒体的热门话题趋势具有挑战性，对于维持动态内容生态至关重要，但现有文献中缺乏面向短视频平台的生产级端到端系统。

Method: 系统整合了三个核心模块：(1)多模态主题提取——从视频的视觉、音频和文本信号中提取主题；(2)时间序列突发检测——识别主题在时间维度上的异常增长；(3)LLM-based整合与增强——利用大语言模型对检测到的趋势进行去重、合并和信息丰富化。

Result: 六个月持续离线人工评估表明系统在识别有意义趋势方面具有高精度；该系统已全球部署，应用于内容排序和搜索等下游场景，驱动了内容新鲜度和用户体验的显著提升。

Conclusion: 本文首次提出了一个面向短视频平台的端到端生产级话题趋势检测系统，验证了多模态融合与LLM增强的有效性，并为社交平台内容生态的动态维护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automatic detection of topical trends at scale is both challenging and essential for maintaining a dynamic content ecosystem on social media platforms. In this work, we present a large-scale system for identifying emerging topical trends on Snapchat, one of the world's largest short-video social platforms. Our system integrates multimodal topic extraction, time-series burst detection, and LLM-based consolidation and enrichment to enable accurate and timely trend discovery. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first published end-to-end system for topical trend detection on short-video platforms at production scale. Continuous offline human evaluation over six months demonstrates high precision in identifying meaningful trends. The system has been deployed in production at global scale and applied to downstream surfaces including content ranking and search, driving measurable improvements in content freshness and user experience.

</details>


### [122] [RAQG-QPP: Query Performance Prediction with Retrieved Query Variants and Retrieval Augmented Query Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27244)
*Fangzheng Tian,Debasis Ganguly,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RAQG方法，利用检索到的历史查询和LLM生成的查询变体来改进神经排序模型的查询性能预测（QPP），在TREC DL'19和DL'20上相比现有方法提升高达30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于查询变体的QPP方法依赖于输入查询的术语扩展生成变体，容易产生不连贯、幻觉化和离题的查询变体，导致预测准确性受限。

Method: 从历史查询日志中检索相关查询作为查询变体，并进一步利用大语言模型（LLM）以检索到的查询变体为条件生成新的查询变体，从而克服仅依赖日志中已有查询的局限性。

Result: 在TREC DL'19和DL'20数据集上，RAQG增强的QPP在MonoT5等神经排序模型上的表现比现有最优的基于查询变体的预测方法提升高达30%。

Conclusion: 结合检索历史查询和LLM生成查询变体的策略能有效提升神经排序模型上的QPP性能。

Abstract: Query Performance Prediction (QPP) estimates the retrieval quality of ranking models without the use of any human-assessed relevance judgements, and finds applications in query-specific selective decision making to improve overall retrieval effectiveness. Although unsupervised QPP approaches are effective for lexical retrieval models, they usually perform weaker for neural rankers. Recent work shows that leveraging query variants (QVs), i.e., queries with potentially similar information needs to a given query, can enhance unsupervised QPP accuracy. However, existing QV-based prediction methods rely on query variants generated by term expansion of the input query, which is likely to yield incoherent, hallucinatory and off-topic QVs. In this paper, we propose to make use of queries retrieved from a log of past queries as QVs to be subsequently used for QPP. In addition to directly applying retrieved QVs in QPP, we further propose to leverage large language models (LLMs) to generate QVs conditioned on the retrieved QVs, thus mitigating the limitation of relying only on existing queries in a log. Experiments on TREC DL'19 and DL'20 show that QPP enhanced with RAQG outperform the best-performing existing QV-based prediction approach by as much as 30% on neural ranking models such as MonoT5.

</details>


### [123] [NuggetIndex: Governed Atomic Retrieval for Maintainable RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27306)
*Saber Zerhoudi,Michael Granitzer,Jelena Mitrovic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出NuggetIndex，一种以原子信息单元（nuggets）为管理记录的检索系统，通过维护证据链接、时间有效性区间和生命周期状态，在排序前过滤无效或废弃的nuggets，从而提升RAG系统的检索质量、时间正确性和事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统存在评估与检索对象之间的单位不匹配问题：评估基于事实指标，但检索的是段落或静态命题。这种不匹配导致语料库演进时维护困难，且无法处理过时事实或来源冲突。

Method: 提出NuggetIndex，将原子信息单元（nuggets）作为管理记录进行存储，每条记录维护证据链接、时间有效性区间和生命周期状态；在排序前过滤无效或废弃的nuggets，防止过时信息被纳入生成过程。

Result: 在三个基准上评估：nugget召回率提升42%，时间正确性提升9个百分点且无时间过滤基线中的召回崩溃，冲突率降低55%；生成器输入长度减少64%，支持轻量级索引结构。

Conclusion: NuggetIndex通过原子化信息单元管理有效解决了RAG系统中评估与检索对象不匹配问题，显著提升检索质量、时间正确性和事实一致性，同时降低计算开销，适用于资源受限场景。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are frequently evaluated via fact-based metrics, yet standard implementations retrieve passages or static propositions. This unit mismatch between evaluation and retrieval objects hinders maintenance when corpora evolve and fails to capture superseded facts or source disagreements. We propose NuggetIndex, a retrieval system that stores atomic information units as managed records, so called nuggets. Each record maintains links to evidence, a temporal validity interval, and a lifecycle state. By filtering invalid or deprecated nuggets prior to ranking, the system prevents the inclusion of outdated information. We evaluate the approach using a nuggetized MS MARCO subset, a temporal Wikipedia QA dataset, and a multi-hop QA task. Against passage and unmanaged proposition retrieval baselines, NuggetIndex improves nugget recall by 42%, increases temporal correctness by 9 percentage points without the recall collapse observed in time-filtered baselines, and reduces conflict rates by 55%. The compact nugget format reduces generator input length by 64% while enabling lightweight index structures suitable for browser-based and resource-constrained deployment. We release our implementation, datasets, and evaluation scripts

</details>


### [124] [From Unstructured to Structured: LLM-Guided Attribute Graphs for Entity Search and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27410)
*Yilun Zhu,Nikhita Vedula,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合大语言模型属性图构建和图感知LLM排序的两阶段方法，在零样本场景下无需训练数据即可提升电商实体搜索精度5%以上，同时将每产品token使用量减少57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商场景中实体搜索面临产品相似性跨类别和跨上下文变化的独特挑战，传统嵌入方法难以捕捉细粒度的上下文特定属性相关性。

Method: 两阶段方法：离线阶段利用LLM从非结构化文本中提取结构化产品属性，构建带有类别感知模式的属性图；在线阶段基于该结构化表示而非原始文本对检索候选进行图感知LLM排序。

Result: 零样本场景下平均精度提升超过5%，每产品token使用量减少57%，在多种产品类别上表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该方法无需训练数据即可在多个基线上取得优势，具备良好的跨类别泛化能力和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Entity search, i.e., finding the most similar entities to a query entity, faces unique challenges in e-commerce, where product similarity varies across categories and contexts. Traditional embedding-based approaches often struggle to capture nuanced context-specific attribute relevance. In this paper, we present a two-stage approach combining Large Language Model (LLM)-driven attribute graph construction with graph-aware LLM ranking. In the offline stage, we extract structured product attributes from unstructured text, and construct a reusable attribute graph with category-aware schemas. In the online stage, we rank retrieved candidates by reasoning over this structured representation rather than raw text, reducing per-product token usage by 57% while improving ranking precision. Experiments show that our approach outperforms multiple baselines under zero-shot scenarios, achieving a over 5% improvement in average precision without requiring training data, generalizes robustly across diverse product categories, and shows immense potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [125] [A Reproducibility Study of LLM-Based Query Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27421)
*Amin Bigdeli,Radin Hamidi Rad,Hai Son Le,Mert Incesu,Negar Arabzadeh,Charles L. A. Clarke,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文在统一可控的实验框架下，系统性地复现并比较了十种基于LLM的查询改写与扩展方法，发现检索范式对改写收益有决定性影响，且神经检索器上的增益不显著，大模型并不总能带来更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM查询改写在异构实验条件下报告显著增益，但缺乏统一的复现与对比分析，难以判断哪些结论可靠、哪些取决于实现细节。

Method: 在统一严格框架下，对十种代表性LLM查询改写方法进行系统性复现与比较，覆盖两种LLM架构、两个参数规模、三种检索范式（词法、学习型稀疏、稠密）以及九个基准数据集（TREC Deep Learning和BEIR）。

Result: 改写增益强烈依赖于检索范式；词法检索下的改进无法一致迁移到神经检索器；更大LLM并不均匀带来更好的下游性能。

Conclusion: 澄清了先前工作中报告增益的稳定性与局限性，并通过QueryGym开源工具包和公共排行榜促进透明复现与持续比较。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are now widely used for query reformulation and expansion in Information Retrieval, with many studies reporting substantial effectiveness gains. However, these results are typically obtained under heterogeneous experimental conditions, making it difficult to assess which findings are reproducible and which depend on specific implementation choices. In this work, we present a systematic reproducibility and comparative study of ten representative LLM-based query reformulation methods under a unified and strictly controlled experimental framework. We evaluate methods across two architectural LLM families at two parameter scales, three retrieval paradigms (lexical, learned sparse, and dense), and nine benchmark datasets spanning TREC Deep Learning and BEIR. Our results show that reformulation gains are strongly conditioned on the retrieval paradigm, that improvements observed under lexical retrieval do not consistently transfer to neural retrievers, and that larger LLMs do not uniformly yield better downstream performance. These findings clarify the stability and limits of reported gains in prior work. To enable transparent replication and ongoing comparison, we release all prompts, configurations, evaluation scripts, and run files through QueryGym, an open-source reformulation toolkit with a public leaderboard.\footnote{https://leaderboard.querygym.com}

</details>


### [126] [Reproducing Adaptive Reranking for Reasoning-Intensive IR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27577)
*Mandeep Rathee,V Venktesh,Sean MacAvaney,Avishek Anand*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文在BRIGHT推理密集型检索基准上复现了GAR（基于图的适应性重排序）方法，发现通过修改重排序过程而非改进第一阶段检索器，可以缓解检索管道的召回率瓶颈问题。实验表明，GAR能有效提升多种推理型和非推理型重排序模型的性能，且计算开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的检索-重排序级联管道存在第一阶段检索器的有界召回问题，而现有方法通过改进检索器来解决该问题，但训练和推理成本高昂，尤其对于需要大量推理的查询场景。

Method: 在BRIGHT推理密集型检索基准上复现GAR方法，通过迭代探索语料图来修改重排序过程，使用推理型和非推理型重排序模型进行实验验证。

Result: GAR在多种模型上均能提升推理密集型检索的效果，且重排序器的信号质量对在语料图中识别额外相关文档起关键作用。该方法计算开销极小。

Conclusion: GAR使得检索系统能够更实用地部署于处理推理密集型查询的场景，在不显著增加计算负担的前提下有效提升检索性能。

Abstract: The classical cascading pipeline of retrieve--rerank suffers from a bounded recall problem, stemming from limitations of the first-stage retriever. Most current approaches address the bounded recall problem by improving the first-stage retriever, but this incurs substantial training and inference costs, especially to handle queries that require substantial reasoning. To circumvent the computational costs of reasoning-based retrievers, we replicate the findings of GAR, Graph-based Adaptive Reranking, on the BRIGHT reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmark. GAR addresses the bounded recall problem by modifying the reranking process itself through iterative exploration of a corpus graph, but it was previously only tested on models designed for topical and question-answering-style queries. Hence, reproduce GAR in reasoning-intensive settings with reasoning and non-reasoning reranking models. We observe that the quality of the reranker's signal plays an important role in identifying additional relevant documents within the corpus graph. Overall, we find that GAR boosts the effectiveness of reasoning-intensive retrieval across a variety of models while contributing minimally to computational overheads. Ultimately, this work enables more practical deployment of retrieval systems that can address reasoning-intensive queries.

</details>


### [127] [One Pass, Any Order: Position-Invariant Listwise Reranking for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27599)
*Ethan Bito,Yongli Ren,Estrid He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出InvariRank框架，通过架构级设计（结构化注意力掩码和共享位置编码）实现排列不变性，解决了LLM列表式推荐重排中因候选顺序敏感导致的排序不稳定问题，在保持竞争力的同时产生稳定排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在列表式推荐重排中，其序列化计算方式导致对候选顺序敏感，相同的候选集因排列不同而产生不同的评分和排序结果，使基于LLM的重排器难以依赖，需要解决这种顺序依赖性问题。

Method: InvariRank通过结构化注意力掩码阻断跨候选注意力，并结合旋转位置编码（RoPE）下的共享位置框架消除位置引起的评分变化；采用列表式学习排序目标，单次前向传播完成所有候选评分，避免需要多次排列的训练目标。

Result: 在推荐基准测试中，InvariRank在保持竞争力排序效果的同时，在不同候选排列下产生稳定的排序结果。

Conclusion: 架构级不变性是实现可靠且高效的LLM推荐重排的实用途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for recommendation reranking, but their listwise predictions can depend on the order in which candidates are presented. This creates a mismatch between the set-based nature of recommendation and the sequence-based computation of decoder-only LLMs, where permuting an otherwise identical candidate set can change item scores and final rankings. Such order sensitivity makes LLM-based rerankers difficult to rely on, since rankings may reflect prompt serialization rather than user preference. We propose InvariRank, a permutation-invariant listwise reranking framework that addresses this dependence at the architectural level. InvariRank blocks cross-candidate attention with a structured attention mask and negates position-induced scoring changes through shared positional framing under Rotary Positional Embeddings (RoPE). Combined with a listwise learning-to-rank objective, the model scores all candidates in a single forward pass, avoiding permutation-based invariance training objectives that require multiple permutations of a candidate set. Experiments on recommendation benchmarks show that InvariRank maintains competitive ranking effectiveness while producing stable rankings across candidate permutations. The results suggest that architectural invariance is a practical route to reliable and efficient LLM-based recommendation reranking. The source code is at https://github.com/ejbito/InvariRank.

</details>


### [128] [Purifying Multimodal Retrieval: Fragment-Level Evidence Selection for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27600)
*Xihang Wang,Zihan Wang,Chengkai Huang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Quan Z. Sheng,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FES-RAG将多模态检索增强生成重新定义为细粒度证据选择问题，通过选择句子级文本片段和区域级视觉片段而非整个文档，并引入片段信息增益（FIG）指标，实现了更精确的证据选择，在M2RAG基准上CIDEr指标提升高达27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRAG框架将检索到的证据视为不可分割的文档，假设文档内所有内容同等有用，但实际中往往只有小部分内容与查询相关，其余部分引入噪声导致性能下降。本文旨在解决这一根本性局限。

Method: 提出FES-RAG框架：(1) 将多模态文档分解为句子级文本片段和区域级视觉片段；(2) 引入片段信息增益（FIG）度量每个片段对MLLM生成置信度的边际贡献；(3) 基于FIG从高容量MLLM中蒸馏片段级效用判断到轻量级选择器，实现低推理开销的精确证据选择。

Result: 在M2RAG基准上，FES-RAG一致优于最先进的文档级MRAG方法，CIDEr相对提升高达27%，同时使用更少的片段实现了更短上下文长度和更高的事实准确性及生成连贯性。

Conclusion: 通过将MRAG重新定义为细粒度证据选择问题并采用片段级选择策略，FES-RAG有效减少了噪声干扰，提升了生成质量，证明了片段级证据选择的优越性。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MRAG) is widely adopted for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) with external evidence to reduce hallucinations. Despite its success, most existing MRAG frameworks treat retrieved evidence as indivisible documents, implicitly assuming that all content within a document is equally informative. In practice, however, sometimes only a small fraction of a document is relevant to a given query, while the remaining content introduces substantial noise that may lead to performance degradation. We address this fundamental limitation by reframing MRAG as a fine-grained evidence selection problem. We propose Fragment-level Evidence Selection for RAG (FES-RAG), a framework that selects atomic multimodal fragments rather than entire documents as grounding evidence. FES-RAG decomposes retrieved multimodal documents into sentence-level textual fragments and region-level visual fragments, enabling precise identification of evidence that directly supports generation. To guide fragment selection, we introduce Fragment Information Gain (FIG), a principled metric that measures the marginal contribution of each fragment to the MLLM's generation confidence. Based on FIG, we distill fragment-level utility judgments from a high-capacity MLLM into a lightweight selector, achieving accurate evidence selection with low inference overhead. Experiments on the M2RAG benchmark show that FES-RAG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art document-level MRAG methods, achieving up to 27 percent relative improvement in CIDEr. By selecting fewer yet more informative fragments, our approach substantially reduces context length while improving factual accuracy and generation coherence.

</details>


### [129] [Position-Aware Drafting for Inference Acceleration in LLM-Based Generative List-Wise Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27747)
*Jiaju Chen,Chongming Gao,Chenxiao Fan,Haoyan Liu,Qingpeng Cai,Peng Jiang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PAD-Rec提出一种位置感知的推测解码方法，通过引入物品位置嵌入和步骤位置嵌入来增强生成式推荐的推理速度，在四个数据集上实现最高3.1倍加速，相比强基线平均提升约5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的生成式列表推荐中，推测解码将所有token（包括分隔符）统一处理，忽略了(1) token语义依赖于其在物品内的槽位位置，以及(2)不确定性随推测深度增加而增大，这限制了加速效果。

Method: 提出PAD-Rec轻量模块，在草稿模型中增加两种互补信号：物品位置嵌入（编码token在物品内的槽位）和步骤位置嵌入（编码推测步骤，适应深度依赖的不确定性），并通过门控机制（可学习系数和上下文驱动门）与基础特征协调。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，实现最高3.1倍端到端加速，平均相比强SD基线提升约5%的加速，同时基本保持推荐质量。

Conclusion: PAD-Rec是一种轻量、易集成、无额外推理开销的位置感知推测解码方法，有效提升了生成式推荐的推理效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based generative list-wise recommendation has advanced rapidly, but decoding remains sequential and thus latency-prone. To accelerate inference without changing the target distribution, speculative decoding (SD) uses a small draft model to propose several next tokens at once and a target LLM to verify and accept the longest prefix, skipping multiple steps per round. In generative recommendation, however, each item is represented by multiple semantic-ID tokens, often with separators, and current drafts typically treat these tokens uniformly. This overlooks two practical facts: (i) a token's semantics depend on its within-item slot, and (ii) uncertainty tends to increase with speculation depth. Without modeling these effects, SD's speedups can be limited. We introduce PAD-Rec, Position-Aware Drafting for generative Recommendation, a lightweight module that augments the draft model with two complementary signals. Item position embeddings explicitly encode the within-item slot of each token, strengthening structural awareness. Step position embeddings encode the draft step, allowing the model to adapt to depth-dependent uncertainty and improve proposal quality. To harmonize these signals with base features, we add simple gates: a learnable coefficient for item slots and a context-driven gate for draft steps. The module is trainable, easy to integrate with standard draft models, and adds negligible inference overhead. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets show up to 3.1x wall-clock speedup and about 5% average wall-clock speedup gain over strong SD baselines, while largely preserving recommendation quality.

</details>


### [130] [NeocorRAG: Less Irrelevant Information, More Explicit Evidence, and More Effective Recall via Evidence Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27852)
*Shiyao Peng,Qianhe Zheng,Zhuodi Hao,Zichen Tang,Rongjin Li,Qing Huang,Jiayu Huang,Jiacheng Liu,Yifan Zhu,Haihong E*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Recall Conversion Rate (RCR)指标，揭示了检索性能提升与下游推理准确率之间存在的"转化率衰减"问题。针对现有方法在检索质量优化上的不足，提出了NeocorRAG框架，通过证据链挖掘与利用实现检索质量的整体优化，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能，且仅消耗同类方法20%以下的token量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG领域存在一个被忽视的关键问题：检索性能的提升并不能一致地转化为下游推理能力的提升。现有方法要么只关注检索数量（召回率）而忽视检索质量，要么只优化质量但召回表现不佳，两者均缺乏对检索质量优化的全面理解，陷入权衡困境。

Method: 提出NeocorRAG框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 采用创新的激活搜索算法获取精炼的候选空间；(2) 通过约束解码确保证据链的精确生成；(3) 利用检索到的证据链集合指导检索优化过程。同时提出RCR指标量化检索对推理准确率的贡献程度。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA、MuSiQue、NQ等基准测试上，NeocorRAG在3B和70B参数模型上均取得SOTA性能，且token消耗量仅为同类方法的20%以下。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种高效、无需训练的RAG增强范式，能有效优化检索质量同时保持高召回率，为RAG领域的检索-推理鸿沟问题提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Although precise recall is a core objective in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), a critical oversight persists in the field: improvements in retrieval performance do not consistently translate to commensurate gains in downstream reasoning. To diagnose this gap, we propose the Recall Conversion Rate (RCR), a novel evaluation metric to quantify the contribution of retrieval to reasoning accuracy. Our quantitative analysis of mainstream RAG methods reveals that as Recall@5 improves, the RCR exhibits a near-linear decay. We identify the neglect of retrieval quality in these methods as the underlying cause. In contrast, approaches that focus solely on quality optimization often suffer from inferior recall performance. Both categories lack a comprehensive understanding of retrieval quality optimization, resulting in a trade-off dilemma. To address these challenges, we propose comprehensive retrieval quality optimization criteria and introduce the NeocorRAG framework. This framework achieves holistic retrieval quality optimization by systematically mining and utilizing Evidence Chains. Specifically, NeocorRAG first employs an innovative activated search algorithm to obtain a refined candidate space. Then it ensures precise evidence chain generation through constrained decoding. Finally, the retrieved set of evidence chains guides the retrieval optimization process. Evaluated on benchmarks including HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, MuSiQue, and NQ, NeocorRAG achieves SOTA performance on both 3B and 70B parameter models, while consuming less than 20% of tokens used by comparable methods. This study presents an efficient, training-free paradigm for RAG enhancement that effectively optimizes retrieval quality while maintaining high recall. Our code is released at https://github.com/BUPT-Reasoning-Lab/NeocorRAG.

</details>


### [131] [SimEval-IR: A Unified Toolkit and Benchmark Suite for Evaluating User Simulators and Search Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27878)
*Saber Zerhoudi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SimEval-IR 是一个开源的交互式信息检索用户模拟器评估工具包和基准套件，它区分并分别衡量行为真实性和测试者可靠性两个目标，并发现当前主流的分类器-判别器"类人"检验对系统排序有效性几乎没有预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区缺乏标准化的用户模拟器评估工具，且模拟器的两个核心目标——行为真实性和测试者可靠性——常常被混为一谈，尽管它们有时相互冲突。

Method: SimEval-IR 提供了：(1) 统一的会话模式，整合会话搜索和对话交互，附带验证过的数据集适配器和显式损失核算；(2) 三个可执行基准，分别覆盖行为真实性、RATE-style 测试者可靠性估计，以及二者关联分析；(3) 在四个真实数据集（两种语言）和四种模拟器家族上报告基线结果。

Result: 分类器-判别器"类人"检验对系统排序有效性的预测能力极弱（r=+0.09, n=48），而边际点击深度距离和会话嵌入上的 Fréchet 距离提供了显著更强的信号（|r|=0.43 和 0.40, p≤0.005）。

Conclusion: SimEval-IR 以可复现的方式提供了区分性和可操作的评估框架，揭示了现有主流真实性检验方法的局限性，并指明了更有效的替代指标。

Abstract: User simulators are increasingly central to interactive information retrieval, yet the community lacks standardized evaluation tools. Simulators serve two objectives, behavioral realism (matching real user behavior) and tester reliability (producing valid system rankings), and these are often conflated despite being distinct and sometimes conflicting. We present SimEval-IR, an open-source toolkit and benchmark suite that makes this distinction measurable. SimEval-IR provides: (1) a canonical session schema unifying session search and conversational interactions, with validated dataset adapters and explicit loss accounting; (2) three executable benchmarks covering behavioral realism, tester reliability with RATE-style estimation, and an analysis linking the two; and (3) baseline results across four real datasets in two languages and four simulator families. Our key finding: the classifier-discriminator ''human-likeness'' check, the dominant realism test in the literature, has essentially no pooled predictive power for system-ranking validity ($r{=}{+}0.09$, $n{=}48$), while marginal click-depth distance and Fréchet distance over session embeddings give a much stronger signal ($|r|{=}0.43$ and $0.40$, $p{\leq}0.005$). SimEval-IR is released with all configurations and scripts to reproduce the reported analysis.

</details>


### [132] [Efficient Multivector Retrieval with Token-Aware Clustering and Hierarchical Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28142)
*Silvio Martinico,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TACHIOM，一种利用token级结构加速多向量检索的系统。通过考虑token分布进行质心分配，可扩展至百万级质心，结合基于图的索引和优化乘积量化，在MS-MARCOv1和LoTTE上实现比k-means快247倍的聚类速度和比现有系统快9.8倍的检索加速，同时保持相当甚至更优的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量检索模型虽具有细粒度token级表示带来的先进效果，但部署时计算和内存成本过高。现有基于k-means的解决方案在聚类规模扩大时扩展性差，且偏向高频token而低估了稀有但具判别性的token。

Method: TACHIOM利用token级结构：1) 考虑token分布进行质心分配，可扩展至百万级质心；2) 结合基于图的索引和优化的乘积量化(Product Quantization)布局，实现高效的最终评分，避免昂贵的token级计算。

Result: 在MS-MARCOv1和LoTTE数据集上，TACHIOM聚类速度比k-means快247倍，检索速度比现有最优系统快9.8倍，同时保持相当或更优的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过有效利用token分布信息和扩展质心规模，TACHIOM在显著加速聚类和检索的同时，没有牺牲检索质量，为多向量检索的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multivector retrieval models achieve state-of-the-art effectiveness through fine-grained token-level representations, but their deployment incurs substantial computational and memory costs. Current solutions, based on the well-known k-means clustering algorithm, group similar vectors together to enable both effective compression and efficient retrieval. However, standard k-means scales poorly with the number of clusters and dataset size, and favours frequent tokens during training while underrepresenting rare, discriminative ones. In this work, we introduce TACHIOM, a multivector retrieval system that exploits token-level structure to significantly accelerate both clustering and retrieval. By accounting for tokens' distribution during centroid allocation, TACHIOM easily scales to millions of centroids, enabling highly accurate document scoring using only centroids, avoiding expensive token-level computation. TACHIOM combines a graph-based index over centroids with an optimized Product Quantization layout for efficient final scoring. Experiments on MS-MARCOv1 and LoTTE show that TACHIOM achieves up to $247\times$ faster clustering than k-means and up to $9.8\times$ retrieval speedup over state-of-the-art systems while maintaining comparable or superior effectiveness.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Monitoring Neural Training with Topology: A Footprint-Predictable Collapse Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26984)
*Alexander Kalinowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种拓扑感知的在线监控方法（MMHM+CI），用于检测神经表征中的表示坍塌问题，可在下游性能下降前提供早期预警信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表征的表示坍塌（representational collapse）会导致嵌入变得各向异性并失去多尺度结构，但传统性能指标无法及时反映这一问题，需要更早的检测手段。

Method: 将模块化Morse同调维护（MMHM）与复合坍塌指数（CI）相结合，通过稀疏编辑和离散Morse匹配实现快速增量更新，而非每轮重建复形。

Result: 在LLM微调和时序知识图谱嵌入（KGE）训练中，CI提供了低延迟的早期预警信号，适用于训练中的干预。

Conclusion: 该方法能在性能指标反应之前有效检测表征退化，支持在线监控和及时干预。

Abstract: Representational collapse, where embeddings become anisotropic and lose multi-scale structure, can erode downstream performance long before performance metrics react. We propose an online, topology-aware monitor for evolving neural representations that couples Modular Morse Homology Maintenance (MMHM) with a composite Collapse Index (CI). Instead of rebuilding complexes each epoch, we apply sparse edits at a fixed scale and maintain a discrete Morse matching, yielding fast, incremental updates. Across LLM fine-tuning and temporal KGE training, CI provides a low-latency early-warning signal suitable for in-training interventions. Code and experimental scripts will be released publicly

</details>


### [134] [Simple Self-Conditioning Adaptation for Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26985)
*Michael Cardei,Huu Binh Ta,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自条件掩码扩散模型（SCMDM），通过后训练适配使每个去噪步骤利用模型自身先前生成的干净状态预测，无需重新训练模型，在多个领域（文本、图像、分子、基因组）显著提升生成质量，生成困惑度降低约50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码扩散模型中，若某位置在反向更新后仍处于掩码状态，模型会丢弃该位置的干净状态预测，导致需从掩码标记反复推断，限制了跨步骤的精细化能力。

Method: 提出后训练适配方法SCMDM，在每一步去噪时，将模型自身上一轮的干净状态预测作为条件输入，仅需最小架构改动，不引入循环隐状态路径，不依赖辅助参考模型，且不增加采样时的额外去噪评估次数。与需从头训练的50% dropout部分自条件方法不同，SCMDM在模型自身预测变得有效后，专注于细化任务而非混合条件/无条件目标。

Result: OWT训练模型生成困惑度从42.89降至23.72（降低~50%），在离散图像生成、小分子生成、基因组分布建模等任务上均显著优于标准MDM基线。

Conclusion: 后训练自条件化是一种简单高效、广泛适用的MDM改进策略，可取代从头训练的部分自条件方法。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate discrete sequences by iterative denoising under an absorbing masking process. In standard masked diffusion, if a token remains masked after a reverse update, the model discards its clean-state prediction for that position. Thus, still-masked positions must be repeatedly inferred from the mask token alone. This design choice limits cross-step refinement. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a simple, yet effective, post-training adaptation for MDMs that conditions each denoising step on the model's own previous clean-state predictions. The resulting method, called Self-Conditioned Masked Diffusion Models (SCMDM), requires minimal architectural change, does not introduce a recurrent latent-state pathway, does not rely on an auxiliary reference model, and adds no extra denoiser evaluations during sampling. This is an important departure from partial self-conditioning approaches which requires expensive model training from scratch. In particular, the paper shows that partial self-conditioning, including the commonly used 50% dropout strategy for training self-conditioned models from scratch, is suboptimal in the post-training regime. Instead, once the model's self-generated clean-state estimates become informative, the specialization to refinement is preferable to mixing conditional and unconditional objectives. SCMDM is evaluated across multiple domains, demonstrating consistent improvement over vanilla MDM baselines, achieving nearly a 50% reduction in generative perplexity on OWT-trained models (42.89 to 23.72), alongside strong improvements in discretized image synthesis quality, small molecular generation, and enhanced fidelity in genomic distribution modeling.

</details>


### [135] [People-Centred Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26991)
*Zheng Zhang,Milad Masroor,Cuong Nguyen,Tahir Hassan,Yuanhong Chen,David Rosewarne,Kevin Wells,Thanh-Toan Do,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PecMan框架，通过动态门控机制在临床工作量约束下联合优化公平性、诊断准确性和工作流程效能，并引入FairHAI基准来评估准确性、公平性和临床工作量之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的医疗AI虽诊断准确度高，但因忽视跨人群公平性能优化和临床工作流程整合而导致临床采纳率低。前人工作将工作流整合（如L2D、L2C）与AI公平性分开研究，忽视了它们之间的相互依赖性及临床环境中医生可用性受限等实际约束。

Method: 提出People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan)框架，采用动态门控机制，在临床医生工作量约束下将病例分配给AI、临床医生或两者共同处理，联合优化公平性、诊断准确性和工作流效能。

Result: 引入FairHAI基准进行实验评估，PecMan在准确性、公平性和临床工作量之间的权衡上始终优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PecMan为更可信、临床可行的AI系统铺平了道路，表明联合优化公平性与工作流整合是提升医疗AI临床采纳的关键方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in data-centric medical AI have produced highly accurate diagnostic systems, but the emphasis on data curation and performance metrics has not translated into widespread clinical adoption. We conjecture that this limited uptake stems from insufficient attention dedicated to the optimisation of fair performance across diverse patient populations and to workflow integration: performance biases can create regulatory barriers, and poorly integrated automation can disrupt clinical routines, degrade the quality of human-AI collaboration, and reduce clinicians' willingness to adopt AI tools. Prior work on workflow integration (e.g., Learning to Defer (L2D) and Learning to Complement (L2C)) and AI fairness has typically examined these challenges in isolation, overlooking their natural interdependence and the practical constraints of clinical environments, such as restricted clinician availability. We propose People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan), a human-AI framework that jointly optimises fairness, diagnostic accuracy, and workflow effectiveness through a dynamic gating mechanism that assigns cases to AI, clinicians, or both under clinician workload constraints. We also introduce the Fairness and Human-Centred AI (FairHAI) benchmark for evaluating trade-offs between accuracy, fairness, and clinician workload. Experiments using this benchmark show that PecMan consistently outperforms existing methods, paving the way for more trustworthy and clinically viable AI systems. Code will be available upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [136] [Automatic Causal Fairness Analysis with LLM-Generated Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27011)
*Alessia Berarducci,Eric Rossetto,Alessandro Antonucci,Marco Zaffalon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FairMind，一个基于因果反事实推理的自动化数据集公平性分析工具，利用LLM生成公平性报告，并支持有序保护变量和连续目标等扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoML框架大多未考虑训练数据及预测结果中潜在的公平性问题，需要一种自动化方法来分析数据集层面的公平性。

Method: 基于Plečko和Bareinboim提出的标准公平性模型，通过因果反事实查询计算公平性效应，并在数据预处理后实现封闭形式的效应计算；利用LLM在零样本设置下生成公平性分析报告。

Result: 通过示例展示了FairMind相比LLM直接分析在公平性检测方面的优势，并提出了面向有序保护变量和连续目标的新分解结果。

Conclusion: FairMind为AutoML流程中的公平性自动化分析提供了因果驱动的有效解决方案，扩展了标准公平性模型的适用范围。

Abstract: AutoML, intended as the process of automating the application of machine learning to real-world problems, is a key step for AI popularisation. Most AutoML frameworks are not accounting for the potential lack of fairness in the training data and in the corresponding predictions. We introduce \textsc{FairMind}, a software prototype aiming to automatise fairness analysis at the dataset level. We achieve that by resorting to the assumptions of the \emph{standard fairness model}, recently proposed by Plečko and Bareinboim. This allows for a sound fairness evaluation in terms of causal effects, based on \emph{counterfactual} queries involving the target, possibly confounders and mediators, and the different values of an input feature we regard as \emph{protected}. After the necessary data preprocessing, the tool implements a closed-form computation of the effects. LLMs are consequently exploited to generate accurate reports on the fairness levels detected in the training dataset. We achieve that in a zero-shot setup and show by examples the expected advantages with respect to a direct analysis performed by the LLM. To favour applications, extensions to ordinal protected variable and continuous targets and novel decomposition results are also discussed.

</details>


### [137] [Fidelity, Diversity, and Privacy: A Multi-Dimensional LLM Evaluation for Clinical Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27014)
*Guillermo Iglesias,Gema Bello-Orgaz,María Navas-Loro,Cristian Ramirez-Atencia,Mercè Salvador Robert,Enrique Baca-Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决医疗数据稀缺和隐私限制问题，利用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5等大型语言模型生成基于ICD-10编码的合成精神健康评估报告，并从语义保真度、词汇多样性和隐私/抄袭三个维度进行评估，证明该方法能生成临床连贯、多样且隐私安全的合成数据，有效扩展临床NLP训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量标注医学数据（尤其是精神健康领域）极度匮乏，隐私法规限制了数据共享，因此需要探索合成数据生成作为替代方案以突破训练数据瓶颈。

Method: 使用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5三种LLM，基于特定ICD-10编码条件生成合成精神健康评估报告，并引入包含语义保真度、词汇多样性、隐私/抄袭三个维度的综合评估框架。

Result: 所有模型均能生成临床连贯、词汇多样且隐私安全的合成报告，有效扩展了可用于临床NLP任务的训练数据，且未侵犯患者机密性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的合成数据生成是解决医疗数据稀缺和有隐私限制场景下数据扩充的有效方案，尤其在精神健康领域具有良好应用前景。

Abstract: The scarcity of high-quality annotated medical data, particularly in mental health, poses a significant bottleneck for training robust machine learning models. Privacy regulations restrict data sharing, making synthetic data generation a promising alternative. The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in a data augmentation pipeline could be leveraged as an alternative in this field. In the proposed methodology, DeepSeek-R1, OpenBioLLM-Llama3 and Qwen 3.5 are used to generate synthetic mental health evaluation reports conditioned on specific International Classification of Diseases, Tenth Revision (ICD-10) codes. Because naive text generation can lead to mode collapse or privacy breaches (memorization), a comprehensive evaluation framework is introduced. The generated diagnostic texts are assessed across three dimensions: semantic fidelity, lexical diversity, and privacy/plagiarism. The results demonstrate that all models can generate clinically coherent, diverse, and privacy-safe synthetic reports, significantly expanding the available training data for clinical natural language processing tasks without compromising patient confidentiality.

</details>


### [138] [Dynamic Adversarial Fine-Tuning Reorganizes Refusal Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27019)
*Wenhao Lan,Shan Li,Junbin Yang,Haihua Shen,Yijun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过测量驱动的方法，在7B骨干模型上研究了SFT和R2D2动态对抗微调对拒绝机制的影响。发现R2D2先完全关闭有害请求（ASR=0），后部分重新打开（ASR升至0.250），而SFT稳定性较差。几何分析表明拒绝载体从后层迁移到前层，有效秩保持在1.23-1.27，支持重组解释而非简单漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽刻画了拒绝方向和对齐鲁棒性，但未解释动态对抗微调如何改变训练过程中的拒绝载体，因此需要揭示这一机制以理解安全对齐的权衡。

Method: 在7B骨干模型上，使用固定源的HarmBench、StrongREJECT和XSTest基准，结合五锚点拒绝几何套件和因果干预，比较SFT与R2D2动态对抗微调的表现。

Result: R2D2在步骤50和100时HarmBench ASR降至0.000，后在步骤250和500分别升至0.035和0.250；SFT的ASR维持在0.505-0.588。R2D2的XSTest拒绝率从1.000降至0.228。几何上拒绝载体从后层迁移至前层，有效秩约1.23-1.27。因果干预表明存在低维但效用耦合的控制。

Conclusion: 结果支持拒绝机制的重组解释而非简单漂移，但证据局限于单个骨干模型和固定源攻击。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models must refuse harmful requests without collapsing into broad over-refusal, but the training-time mechanisms behind this tradeoff remain unclear. Prior work characterizes refusal directions and jailbreak robustness, yet does not explain how dynamic adversarial fine-tuning changes refusal carriers across training. We present a measurement-driven mechanism study, not a new defense, on one 7B backbone under supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and R2D2-style dynamic adversarial fine-tuning. Our protocol aligns fixed-source HarmBench, StrongREJECT, and XSTest with a five-anchor refusal-geometry suite and causal interventions. R2D2 drives fixed-source HarmBench ASR to 0.000 at steps 50 and 100, then partially reopens to 0.035 at step 250 and 0.250 at step 500; SFT remains less robust, with ASR between 0.505 and 0.588 at the same anchors. On XSTest, R2D2 any-refusal is 1.000 early, then falls to 0.664 and 0.228. Geometrically, R2D2 preserves a late-layer admissible carrier through step 100 before relocating to an early-layer carrier, while effective rank remains near 1.23--1.27. Causal interventions indicate low-dimensional but utility-coupled control. These results support a reorganization account rather than a drift-only account, with evidence limited to one backbone and fixed-source attacks.

</details>


### [139] [Cross-Subject Generalization for EEG Decoding: A Survey of Deep Learning Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27033)
*Taida Li,Yujun Yan,Fei Dou,Wenzhan Song,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述系统回顾了面向跨被试脑电解码中个体间差异问题的深度学习方法，将其形式化为多源域适应问题，并按特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦和对比学习四类方法进行梳理，最后讨论了理论局限、被试身份的结构化价值以及EEG基础模型等关键议题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电解码在跨被试场景下面临严重的个体间变异（域偏移问题），现有深度学习方法在此场景下泛化能力不足，亟需系统性梳理已有解决方案并指出未来方向。

Method: 首先将跨被试设置形式化为多源域问题，并定义了严格的被试无关评估协议；然后提出了一个系统的分类法，将现有文献分为特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦和对比学习四个方法族。

Result: 给出了当前跨被试脑电解码深度学习方法的系统性分类整理，涵盖四类主要方法，并指出了现有方法的理论局限性。

Conclusion: 推进鲁棒、真实世界脑电解码的三个关键要素：当前方法的理论局限性、被试身份的结构化价值、以及EEG基础模型的兴起。

Abstract: Deep learning for cross-subject EEG decoding is hindered by high inter-subject variability, which introduces a severe domain shift between training and unseen test subjects. This survey presents a comprehensive review of deep learning methodologies specifically engineered to address this cross-subject generalization challenge. To ground this analysis, we formalize the cross-subject setting as a multi-source domain problem and delineate the rigorous, subject-independent evaluation protocols required for valid assessment. Central to this survey is a systematic taxonomy of the current literature into discrete methodological families, including feature alignment, adversarial learning, feature disentanglement, and contrastive learning. We conclude by examining three critical elements for advancing robust, real-world decoding: the theoretical limitations of current methodologies, the structural value of subject identity, and the emergence of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [140] [Detecting Clinical Discrepancies in Health Coaching Agents: A Dual-Stream Memory and Reconciliation Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27045)
*Samuel L Pugh,Eric Yang,Alexander Muir Sutherland,Alessandra Breschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双流记忆架构（Dual-Stream Memory Architecture），将患者自述与结构化临床记录（FHIR）分离存储，并通过 Reconciliation Engine 检测两者间的不一致。在675次纵向健康辅导会话测试中，该引擎检测出84.4%的临床差异，安全关键召回率86.7%，并发现13.6%的误差级联源于非结构化对话中的记忆提取阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用型LLM智能体记忆系统倾向于用用户最新陈述覆盖旧事实以追求连贯性，这在临床数据中会导致安全风险。患者自述虽及时但存在回忆偏差，电子健康记录（EHR）虽经医学验证但常过时，亟需一种能调和两种不完美信息源的记忆架构。

Method: 提出双流记忆架构，严格分离患者叙事与结构化临床记录（FHIR），并由 Reconciliation Engine 对每条提取的记忆进行FHIR档案比对，按类型、严重程度及涉及的FHIR资源对差异进行分类。使用26名患者、675次纵向健康辅导会话的混合数据集（真实医患对话+合成FHIR临床场景）进行评测。

Result: 隔离测试中，引擎检测出84.4%的设计临床差异，安全关键召回率86.7%。通过将提取与调和评估耦合，定量发现13.6%的误差级联，且误差主要源自非结构化会话记忆提取过程中丢失的临床细节，而非下游分类错误。

Conclusion: 验证了将患者报告记忆与临床记录进行比对是可行的，且对于纵向健康智能体的安全部署是必要的。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents transition from single-session tools to persistent systems managing longitudinal healthcare journeys, their memory architectures face a critical challenge: reconciling two imperfect sources of truth. The patient's evolving self-report is current but prone to recall bias, while the Electronic Health Record (EHR) is medically validated but frequently stale. General-purpose agent memory systems optimize for coherence by overwriting older facts with the user's latest statement, a pattern that risks safety failures when applied to clinical data. We introduce a Dual-Stream Memory Architecture that strictly separates the patient narrative from the structured clinical record (FHIR), governed by a dedicated Reconciliation Engine that evaluates every extracted memory against the patient's FHIR profile and classifies discrepancies by type, severity, and the specific FHIR resources involved. We evaluate this architecture on 26 patients across 675 longitudinal wellness coaching sessions, using a hybrid dataset that interleaves real provider-patient transcripts with synthetic, FHIR-grounded clinical scenarios. In isolated testing, the engine detects 84.4% of designed clinical discrepancies with 86.7% safety-critical recall. By coupling extraction and reconciliation evaluation on the same data, we directly quantify a 13.6% error cascade, tracing the degradation to clinical details lost during memory extraction from unstructured conversation rather than to downstream classification errors. These findings establish that validating patient-reported memories against clinical records is both feasible and necessary for safe deployment of longitudinal health agents.

</details>


### [141] [Learning Rate Transfer in Normalized Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27077)
*Boris Shigida,Boris Hanin,Andrey Gromov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出νGPT，一种新的参数化方法，解决了nGPT在学习率跨模型宽度、深度和序列长度迁移上的问题。通过结合数值实验和对齐指数（alignment exponents）原理，改进了μP超参数迁移方法，使νGPT在不同规模模型间实现学习率迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: nGPT虽然在训练加速上表现优异，且无需权重衰减或学习率预热，但其超参数无法跨模型维度和token序列长度迁移。具体表现为学习率在不同模型宽度和Token规模下不具备可迁移性。

Method: 将数值实验与对齐指数（alignment exponents）原理相结合，重新审视并修改μP超参数迁移方法，提出新的参数化方案νGPT。

Result: νGPT在跨宽度、深度和token序列长度上均表现出学习率迁移能力。

Conclusion: 通过大量实验验证，νGPT成功实现了超参数（尤其是学习率）在不同模型规模下的有效迁移，克服了nGPT的局限。

Abstract: The Normalized Transformer, or nGPT (arXiv:2410.01131) achieves impressive training speedups and does not require weight decay or learning rate warmup. However, despite having hyperparameters that explicitly scale with model size, we observe that nGPT does not exhibit learning rate transfer across model dimension and token horizon. To rectify this, we combine numerical experiments with a principled use of alignment exponents (arXiv:2407.05872) to revisit and modify the $μ$P approach to hyperparameter transfer (arXiv:2011.14522). The result is a novel nGPT parameterization we call $ν$GPT. Through extensive empirical validation, we find $ν$GPT exhibits learning rate transfer across width, depth, and token horizon.

</details>


### [142] [Co-Evolving Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27083)
*Naibin Gu,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Chuanyu Qin,Dingyu Yao,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoPD（共进化策略蒸馏）方法，通过并行训练专家并在RLVR训练过程中引入双向OPD，解决了混合RLVR的能力冲突和传统OPD的行为模式差异问题，实现了文本、图像、视频推理能力的一体化整合，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种后训练范式（混合RLVR和先训练专家再OPD）在整合多专家能力时各有缺陷：混合RLVR存在跨能力发散损失，而流水线式OPD因师生行为模式差距大导致无法充分吸收教师能力。需要一种能同时避免这两类损失的新方法。

Method: 提出CoPD（Co-Evolving Policy Distillation），核心创新为：(1) 并行训练多个专家；(2) 在每个专家进行RLVR训练的过程中（而非训练完成后）引入OPD；(3) 专家之间互为教师，实现双向蒸馏（bidirectional OPD），使专家间行为模式更一致同时保持互补知识。

Result: 实验证明CoPD实现了文本、图像、视频推理能力的一体化整合，显著优于混合RLVR和MOPD等强基线方法，甚至超越了领域特定的专家模型。

Conclusion: CoPD提供了一种新的模型并行训练范式，有望启发新的训练扩展规律（training scaling paradigm），有效解决多能力整合中的能力损失问题。

Abstract: RLVR and OPD have become standard paradigms for post-training. We provide a unified analysis of these two paradigms in consolidating multiple expert capabilities into a single model, identifying capability loss in different ways: mixed RLVR suffers from inter-capability divergence cost, while the pipeline of first training experts and then performing OPD, though avoiding divergence, fails to fully absorb teacher capabilities due to large behavioral pattern gaps between teacher and student. We propose Co-Evolving Policy Distillation (CoPD), which encourages parallel training of experts and introduces OPD during each expert's ongoing RLVR training rather than after complete expert training, with experts serving as mutual teachers (making OPD bidirectional) to co-evolve. This enables more consistent behavioral patterns among experts while maintaining sufficient complementary knowledge throughout. Experiments validate that CoPD achieves all-in-one integration of text, image, and video reasoning capabilities, significantly outperforming strong baselines such as mixed RLVR and MOPD, and even surpassing domain-specific experts. The model parallel training pattern offered by CoPD may inspire a novel training scaling paradigm.

</details>


### [143] [AutoSP: Unlocking Long-Context LLM Training Via Compiler-Based Sequence Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27089)
*Ahan Gupta,Zhihao Wang,Neel Dani,Masahiro Tanaka,Olatunji Ruwase,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoSP是首个自动优化LLM长上下文训练的方案，通过自动序列并行和长上下文感知的激活检查点技术，在NVIDIA和AMD硬件上分别将训练上下文长度提升2.7倍和2.5倍，且几乎不损失运行时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练库缺乏对长上下文训练优化的易用抽象，主要关注大参数模型优化（如ZeRO-3/FSDP、张量并行和流水线并行），用户需重写训练库以集成序列并行等复杂优化，过程需深度专业知识，降低了开发效率。

Method: AutoSP编译模型并应用一组针对性优化：自动化序列并行（自动SP）和长上下文感知的激活检查点（activation-checkpointing），以增强LLM的可训练性。

Result: 在NVIDIA硬件上训练上下文长度提升2.7倍，在AMD硬件上提升2.5倍，且运行时性能损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: AutoSP作为首个自动化长上下文训练优化方案，有效降低了开发者门槛，显著提升了LLM在长上下文场景下的训练能力，并在多种硬件平台上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Large-language-models (LLMs) demonstrate enormous utility in long-context tasks which require processing prompts that consist of tens to hundreds of thousands of tokens. However, existing LLM training libraries do not provide easy to use abstractions to optimize for long-context training, instead focusing on optimizations for models with large parameter counts through ZeRO-3/FSDP, Tensor and Pipeline parallelism. This forces users to rewrite LLM training libraries to incorporate compositions of various complex long-context optimizations, such as sequence-parallelism, to training pipelines; a process that requires in-depth expertise, reducing developer productivity. To tackle these challenges, we introduce AutoSP: the first automated solution to automatically optimize LLM training for longer-contexts. AutoSP compiles models and applies a targeted set of optimizations: automated sequence parallelism, and long-context aware activation-checkpointing, to drastically enhance LLM trainability at negligible cost to throughput. Our evaluation demonstrates AutoSP's capability on both NVIDIA and AMD hardware, increasing training contexts by upto 2.7$\times$ and 2.5$\times$ respectively over competitive hand-written baseline at negligible cost to runtime performance.

</details>


### [144] [Anomaly Detection in Soil Heavy Metal Contamination Using Unsupervised Learning for Environmental Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27102)
*Isaac Tettey Adjokatse,Samuel Senyo Koranteng,George Yamoah Afrifa,Theophilus Ansah-Narh,Marcellin Atemkeng,Joseph Bremang Tandoh,Kow Ahor Essel-Yorke,Richmond Opoku-Sarkodie,Rebecca Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究应用无监督机器学习框架（Isolation Forest、PCA重构误差、DBSCAN及共识方法）检测加纳中部地区垃圾场土壤重金属异常污染模式。共识方法识别出6个稳健异常样本（7.7%），均集中于单一站点（S3），其Hazard Index均值比正常样本高约70%-80%，且均超过HI=1阈值。PCA重构误差与HI呈强正相关（r≈0.8）。识别出三种异常类型：S3处极端Cu富集、S4/S5处异常低Ni、S9-S12处中等多金属（Pb-Zn）共升高。研究表明无监督机器学习可提供超越聚合指标的精细客观洞见，支持靶向场地优先级排序与风险知情环境管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加纳快速城市化地区（尤其是非正规垃圾场）的重金属土壤污染持续威胁环境与公众健康，现有研究多依赖聚合健康风险指数，缺乏对异常污染模式的精细识别与分类，难以实现靶向治理与优先级排序。

Method: 采集加纳中部地区12个垃圾场及住宅对照点共78份土壤样本，检测As、Cd、Cr、Cu、Hg、Ni、Pb、Zn八种金属浓度，并计算Hazard Index (HI)和Incremental Lifetime Cancer Risk (ILCR)。应用四种无监督学习算法：Isolation Forest、PCA重构误差、DBSCAN以及共识方法，检测和分类异常污染模式。

Result: Isolation Forest与PCA重构误差各识别12个异常样本（15.4%），DBSCAN未检出密度隔离噪声点。共识方法识别6个稳健异常（7.7%），全部集中于S3站点。异常样本HI均值高于正常样本约70-80%，所有共识异常HI>1。PCA重构误差与HI强相关（r≈0.8）。识别出三种异常类型：S3极端Cu富集、S4/S5异常低Ni、S9-S12中等Pb-Zn共升高。

Conclusion: 无监督机器学习方法能超越传统聚合健康风险指数，提供更精细、客观的异常污染模式识别与分类能力，有助于实现靶向场地优先级排序与风险知情环境管理决策。

Abstract: Soil contamination by heavy metals poses a persistent environmental and public health concern in rapidly urbanising regions of Ghana, particularly at unregulated waste disposal sites. This study applies an unsupervised machine learning framework to detect and characterise anomalous heavy metal contamination patterns in soils from twelve waste sites and residential controls in the Central Region, of Ghana. Concentrations of eight metals (As, Cd, Cr, Cu, Hg, Ni, Pb, Zn) were analysed alongside standard health risk indices, including the Hazard Index (HI) and Incremental Lifetime Cancer Risk (ILCR). Isolation Forest and PCA reconstruction error each identified $12$ anomalous samples ($15.4\%$ of $78$ samples), while DBSCAN detected no density-isolated noise points. A consensus approach isolated six robust anomalies ($7.7\%)$, all spatially concentrated at a single site (S3). Anomalies exhibited approximately $70$--$80\%$ higher mean HI values than normal samples, with all consensus anomalies exceeding the HI$=1$ threshold. PCA reconstruction error showed a strong positive association with HI ($r \approx 0.8$), indicating consistency between multivariate deviation and health risk. Three distinct anomaly types were identified: extreme Cu enrichment at S3, anomalously low Ni at S4/S5, and moderate multi-metal (Pb--Zn) co-elevation at S9--S12. The results demonstrate that unsupervised machine learning provides granular, objective insight beyond aggregate indices, enabling targeted site prioritisation and risk-informed environmental management.

</details>


### [145] [Better Models, Faster Training: Sigmoid Attention for single-cell Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27124)
*Vijay Sadashivaiah,Georgios Dasoulas,Judith Mueller,Soumya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用Sigmoid注意力机制替代Softmax注意力机制来训练稳定的生物基础模型，在6个单细胞数据集上实现了更高的细胞类型分离度（25%提升）、更快的训练速度（最高10%）和更稳定的训练过程，并开源了高效的TritonSigmoid GPU内核（H100上达515 TFLOPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax注意力机制在生物基础模型训练中存在固有不稳定性，导致梯度爆炸和训练发散问题，需要重新设计适合生物序列的注意力机制。

Method: 提出了Sigmoid注意力机制替代Softmax注意力，其具有全局有界导数（≤0.25）和对角雅可比结构，避免了Softmax的密集耦合；并实现了高效TritonSigmoid GPU内核，支持原生填充。

Result: 在6个单细胞数据集上细胞类型分离度提升25%；训练速度提升最高10%；在无梯度裁剪的1.6亿参数双向注意力模型压力测试中，Softmax梯度爆炸四个数量级而Sigmoid保持稳定；TritonSigmoid在H100上达到515 TFLOPS，超越FlashAttention-2和FlashSigmoid。

Conclusion: Sigmoid注意力在理论上和实验上均优于Softmax注意力，是生物基础模型的更优选择。

Abstract: Training stable biological foundation models requires rethinking attention mechanisms: we find that using sigmoid attention as a drop in replacement for softmax attention a) produces better learned representations: on six diverse single-cell datasets, sigmoid achieves 25% higher cell-type separation, better cell-type cohesion metrics, and lower validation loss, b) faster training, models with sigmoid attention train up to 10% faster than their softmax counterparts, and c) more stable training by eliminating inherent sources of instability in softmax attention. We establish that sigmoid attention has globally bounded derivatives ($\leq 0.25$) as opposed to softmax, and a diagonal Jacobian structure in contrast with softmax's dense coupling, which together help alleviate training instabilities. In stress tests on 160M-parameter bidirectional attention models trained without gradient clipping on 8K-token sequences, softmax diverges catastrophically, with gradients exploding by four orders of magnitude, while sigmoid remains stable. Finally, we implement and open-source TritonSigmoid, an efficient GPU kernel that achieves 515 TFLOPS on H100 GPUs, outperforming both FlashAttention-2 and FlashSigmoid, with native padding support, which is essential for biological sequences. Our results establish sigmoid attention as both theoretically grounded and empirically superior for biological foundation models. Code is available at https://github.com/MSDLLCpapers/triton-sigmoid

</details>


### [146] [How to Guide Your Flow: Few-Step Alignment via Flow Map Reward Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27147)
*Jerry Y. Huang,Justin Lin,Sheel Shah,Kartik Nair,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Map Reward Guidance (FMRG)，将引导问题重新表述为确定性最优控制问题，利用流图实现无需训练、单轨迹的高效生成引导，在文本到图像任务中以仅3次NFE匹配或超越基线，实现至少一个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引导方法要么需要昂贵的多粒子、多步方案，要么依赖理解不充分的近似，缺乏高效且理论清晰的解决方案。

Method: 将引导重新表述为确定性最优控制问题，得到算法层次结构（在最粗层次涵盖现有方法）；利用流图（flow map）作为最优解中的自然产物，提出FMRG框架，使用流图同时进行积分和引导，实现无需训练、单轨迹的生成。

Result: 在文本到图像尺度上，FMRG在逆问题、风格迁移、人类偏好和VLM奖励等任务中匹配或超越基线，仅需3次NFE，相比先前最优方法实现至少一个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: 将引导问题与最优控制理论建立联系，流图在最优解中自然出现，FMRG实现了高效且无需训练的单轨迹引导，大幅提升推理速度。

Abstract: In generative modeling, we often wish to produce samples that maximize a user-specified reward such as aesthetic quality or alignment with human preferences, a problem known as guidance. Despite their widespread use, existing guidance methods either require expensive multi-particle, many-step schemes or rely on poorly understood approximations. We reformulate guidance as a deterministic optimal control problem, yielding a hierarchy of algorithms that subsumes existing approaches at the coarsest level. We show that the flow map, an object of significant recent interest for its role in fast inference, arises naturally in the optimal solution. Based on this observation, we propose Flow Map Reward Guidance (FMRG): a training-free, single-trajectory framework that uses the flow map to both integrate and guide the flow. At text-to-image scale, FMRG matches or surpasses baselines across inverse problems, style transfer, human preferences, and VLM rewards with as few as 3 NFEs, giving at least an order-of-magnitude speedup in comparison to prior state of the art.

</details>


### [147] [Generalizing the Geometry of Model Merging Through Frechet Averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27155)
*Marvin F. da Silva,Mohammed Adnan,Felix Dangel,Sageev Oore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模型合并应使用对称不变的Fréchet平均（在合适流形上最小化测地距离之和），证明了该框架包含Fisher合并，并针对LoRA的商流形几何提出了实用合并算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数空间平均方法未考虑架构对称性，导致合并结果脆弱且次优。需要一种能显式保持对称不变性的合并框架。

Method: 将模型合并形式化为流形上的Fréchet平均——最小化参数点到各模型测地距离的平方和。核心在于流形、度量和距离近似（即"几何"）的选择。通过简化假设证明该框架可退化为Fisher合并。针对LoRA的对称性构造商流形几何，并提出具体合并算法。

Result: 理论证明了Fréchet平均框架包含Fisher合并；分析了当前LoRA合并方法在商流形几何下的局限性；提出的LoRA合并算法与其他常用方法性能相当或更优。

Conclusion: 对称感知的合并必须同时考虑几何和平均过程的对称不变性；Fréchet平均为模型合并提供了统一的理论框架；LoRA的商流形几何为设计高效合并算法提供了新思路。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine multiple models into one without additional training. Naïve parameter-space averaging can be fragile under architectural symmetries, as their geometry does not take them into account. In this work we show that not only the geometry, but also the averaging procedure itself, must be symmetry-invariant to achieve symmetry-aware merges. Consequently, we propose a general solution: merging as Fréchet averaging, i.e., selecting parameters that minimize a sum of geodesic distances on an appropriate manifold. In this view, the key design choice is the overall geometry, i.e., the choice of metric, manifold, and distance approximation, that determines what it means for two models to be "close".
